- Red first: `internal/attackpath/noise_precision_test.go` (fixtures from the noise hop lists) — vet red `undefined: Exposure`; after adding the type, 5 tests red: TP3 not reported behind 40 exposures; 6 exposure-only nodes reported as paths (www 2.00, public-pii-redacted 10.00, sandbox-fixtures 10.00, GCE api-1 4.00, eks-dev 2.00, customer-portal 8.00), Criticality(customer-portal)=4, (api-1)=3; tenant/invoice Lambda + org-conditioned S3 + Azure halyardbackups reported (7.00/10.00), no confidence hint, rule engine kept PUBLIC_NETWORK_EXPOSURE; auth-LB 8.00 = unauthenticated 8.00, toxic, no chip; orphaned user 5.00 path + entry point, unknown-credential user 5.00 not 2.5. `internal/catalog/storage_alias_test.go` red: 10 alias errors (TLS → AZ_STORAGE_PUBLIC_BLOB; CKV_AZURE_59 / AVD-AZU-0012 / scoutsuite public-traffic / steampipe default_network → PUBLIC_DATA_STORE; CKV_GCP_29 / AVD-GCP-0002 / steampipe uniform_access → GCP_BUCKET_PUBLIC). All green after the changes below.
- Decision 6 (TP3) root cause: not the DynamoDB class (aws_dynamodb_table is datastore, sensitive, crit 4) nor grants_access_to. The internet node has 45 exploitable out-edges; `MaxBranching` 25 capped it, so exposures after the 25th in store order were dropped (S3 chain happened to sort earlier). Fix traversal.go:693 (internet exempt). Uncapping surfaced 2 hidden FPs (public-pii-redacted-reports 10.00, halyard-sandbox-public-fixtures 5.00: name-heuristic `sensitive` — "pii", "sandbox" contains "db") — closed by the explicit-classification rule.
- Decision 1/3b: Criticality rewritten (traversal.go:140): configured jewel → tier; data store sensitive (explicit classification wins over name heuristics, `dataClassification`/`sensitiveData` :176/:206) 4 prod / 3; registry Crown data store 3; admin-level identity (toxic.go adminLevel + aws_iam_root) 4; else 0. Built-in name catalog (`*customer*`, `*prod*`, …) removed. `Analysis.Exposures` (exposure.go:35, recorded traversal.go:757) for internet+plumbing chains ending on a non-target. toxic_test case "wide + non-admin jewel" (payments-reader, 3.8 by name) updated to "no path" — deliberate rule change.
- Decision 2: exposure.go `exposureAt`/`anonymousReach`/`policyReach` (:85/:107/:172): auth type AWS_IAM etc. → private "auth required"; every Principal * with aws:PrincipalOrgID/OrgPaths/PrincipalAccount/SourceVpc(e)/SourceAccount → "org-conditioned"; allow_blob_public_access=false / public_network_access=Disabled / S3 BPA on / GCS PAP enforced → "anonymous access disabled"; serverless/object storage without a deciding attribute → `confidence: hint`. Used by evaluate, nodeExposure, managementPort, suppressedExposures, entryWidthOf, AnnotateExposure (labels edge+node private), rule-engine findingExposureSuppressed (:434). Note: the noise findings N049/N050 carry PUBLIC_DATA_STORE in the native JSON, so the catalog fix alone would not have removed the Azure FPs.
- Decision 3: `authFront` (exposure.go:281) — LB/API gateway with authentication/authorizer/OIDC/Cognito or WAF attr → plumbing hop ×0.3 (`AuthLBFactor`), chip `auth-lb ×0.3 (<why>)` in because + evidence.compensating_control; entry no longer toxic-floored (buildPath + fix.go survives :178/:210/:227).
- Decision 4: `credentialState` (traversal.go:485): console true / active key → usable; console_access=false + keys known inactive → no entry; STALE_CREDENTIALS or key age → usable; else hint ×0.5 (`IdentityHintFactor`, evidence.confidence=hint). Implemented on credential state (no console + no active key ⇒ cannot authenticate); the "no policies/groups" clause is implied, not required.
- Decision 5 (catalog.yaml): new AZ_STORAGE_TLS_MIN_VERSION [WEAK_TLS] (prowler storage_ensure_minimum_tls_version_12, checkov CKV_AZURE_44, trivy AVD-AZU-0011) :450; new AZ_STORAGE_NETWORK_UNRESTRICTED [] (CKV_AZURE_59, AVD-AZU-0012, scoutsuite storageaccounts-public-traffic-allowed, steampipe storage_account_default_network_access_rule_denied) :465; new GCP_BUCKET_UNIFORM_ACCESS_DISABLED [] (CKV_GCP_29, AVD-GCP-0002, steampipe storage_bucket_uniform_access_enabled) :657; removed from AZ_STORAGE_PUBLIC_BLOB / GCP_BUCKET_PUBLIC. Not changed (outside the Azure/GCP brief, flagged): S3_PUBLIC_BUCKET aliases CKV_AWS_53–56, prowler s3_account_level_public_access_blocks, steampipe s3_public_access_block_bucket are BPA-configuration checks, not public read; python/nimbus/frameworks/catalog.yaml mirrors the old Azure/GCP aliases.

## Decisions (main session) after V3-cli
- Unparseable/over-limit IaC files block `--fail-on` at EVERY threshold (fail closed), not only HIGH — a PR author must not bypass the gate via `--fail-on critical`; `--fail-on-unscanned=false` remains the explicit opt-out. → integration pass 2 (cli/scan.go uses `output.GateBlocks` semantics).
- M365 Conditional Access: up to 2 individually excluded break-glass users accepted (Microsoft emergency-access guidance); group/role/guest/app exclusions refused. Kept.
- `--auto-pr` drops unverified patches (same as `--require-verify`). Kept.
- Follow-up: M365 token request HTTP client must apply the same redirect credential-stripping as core/rest.go → integration pass 2.

## P1-ENGINE noise round result (main session)
Noise estate: 23/26 FP → 0/4 FP (precision 100%), TPs 3/3 (TP1 7.0, TP3 7.0, TP2 3.8 = P12 gap); labelled lab 12/12 recall, 10/12 @7, precision 100% (eks exposure moved to `Exposures`); strict mode fails only on P12/S01. Root causes fixed: name-pattern criticality removed (impact targets only), anonymous-reach check (IAM auth / org-conditioned → private), auth-LB ×0.3, unusable identities, 9 wrong Azure/GCP catalog aliases, internet-node branching cap (25) silently dropping exposures (revealed 2 more FPs, also fixed).
Remaining: benign CRITICAL/HIGH Issues 28 (severity comes from scanner, not verdict) → integration pass 2 (launched); S3 BPA aliases review; Python catalog sync → pass 2.
- Defect 6 (template `# CLI:` injection): red `TestTemplateCLILinesQuoteHostileNames` (392 renders × 8 hostile values): 22 templates had command lines outside a `CLI:` line, unquoted values in az_storage_public_blob/eks/gcp/rds, option injection (`az storage account update -n --endpoint-url=…`), and 4 payloads executed (PWNED_subst/tick/semi/dq created). Fix: ctx.Raw + template func `sh` (always single-quotes, = shQuote used by shq), every command line rewritten to `# CLI:`/`// CLI:` with `--flag='value'`, `gs://'…'`, `-- 'name'`, placeholders as `"$RESOURCE_GROUP"` etc.; shq quotes a leading `-`; oneLine/yamlLine also map NEL/LS/PS. Green: 256 CLI lines pass bash -n, single-quote span, no option word, run against stubs, no PWNED file.
- Defects 7/8/9 (module guard, yor_trace, shared module): red `TestPatchRefusesUnresolvableModule` (8 module addresses patched the root/other block, incl. the old `module.app` expectation in TestPatchResolvesTarget, now moved), `TestPatchSharedModuleSourceNeedsAllCallers`, `TestYorTraceThroughNonLiteralIsAmbiguous` (10 shapes incl. the trace_local repro patched aws_instance.y). Fix: `PatchTerraformWith(f, root, PatchOptions{AllCallers})`; unresolvable module → TargetError; `moduleIndex.callersOf` refuses a source dir with >1 module call unless AllCallers; Patch.Address keeps the module prefix, new LocalAddress/Callers; trace resolution adds blocks whose tags/tags_all yor_trace is not a literal (traceState, merge last-arg-wins) and module calls carrying it, and refuses when the trace text appears outside the chosen block's tags. Green.
- D6 (dynamic ingress): red `TestDynamicIngressWithOpenCIDRFails` 7/11 subtests (fixv shape SKIP instead of FAIL; literal/toset/iterator/map dynamic open → PASS; unknown for_each → PASS). Fix: iac/dynamic.go expands `dynamic "ingress"` with a static for_each (toset/tolist/tomap/concat/flatten/merge, iterator, ≤10000 instances); unknown for_each/content → SKIP reason. Green 11/11.
- End gate (V3-iac): `go vet ./... && go test ./internal/iac/ ./internal/remediation/ ./internal/output/ -count=1` → exit 0 (iac 18.5 s, remediation 3.6 s, output 0.9 s). `go test ./internal/cli/ ./internal/gitops/ ./internal/catalog/` ok. `FuzzScanHCL -fuzztime 60s` → 744,156 execs, PASS, no new crasher (includes the guard-bound property). CLI re-run of the verify-3 repros with a fresh binary (`scratchpad/v3iac/cp`): cliscan/splat_200000 and cliscan2/bangnl_400000 → no crash, 1 file not scanned; evade/{bad,size,syn} with `--fail-on high` → exit 2, IAC_PARSE_LIMIT in the SARIF (the CLI track already consumes `ScanTerraformResult`). Logs: `scratchpad/v3iac/{red,green}_*.log`, `fuzz60.log`, `final_gate.log`.
- Contract for the CLI track: `iac.ScanTerraformResult(root, now)` → `ScanResult{Root, Findings, UnscannedFiles []string (root-relative, slash, sorted), UnscannedReasons map[file]"<kind>: detail" (kind ∈ syntax|size|depth|read)}` plus `*iac.UnscannedError` when any file is unscanned; one HIGH ERROR `IAC_PARSE_LIMIT` finding per unscanned file tagged `output.TagScanGap`; lifecycle must not resolve stored findings whose File is unscanned in that root. Gate: `output.GateCount/GateBlocks` (scan gaps block every threshold). Patches: `iac.PatchTerraformWith(f, root, iac.PatchOptions{AllCallers})` for `--all-callers`; `Patch.Address` now keeps the module prefix, so `mergePatches` must re-patch with `Patch.LocalAddress`. IaC finding ids changed (root-qualified): stored IaC findings churn once.

- Result per item (red → green; each red run was observed before the fix):
  1. Path Issues wired. Red: `cli/integration2_test.go` `TestPathIssuesWiredAndIssuesCommand` ("scan wrote no path Issues"); server `api_issues2_test.go` `TestPathIssuesAPIHistoryAndSuppress` (`kind=path` → 400), `TestSuppressNeedsServerToken` (405). Fix: `cli/issues.go` (new: `rebuildIssueViews`, `issueVerdict`, `observedScope`, and the `issues` command with list/`show`/`suppress`/`history`). Wiring: `scan.go:505` (scan and ingest via finishScanWith) and `report.go:289` (prioritize). `--diff` uses `lifecycle.go:180` `printPathIssueDiff` and `--diff-json` gets a `path_issues` key. `PathIssueResult` gains `OpenedIDs/ReopenedIDs/ResolvedIDs`. Server (`server.go:108-109,401,570,605`): `kind=path`, `band=`, `state=suppressed`, `GET /api/issues/{id}/history`, `POST /api/issues/{id}/suppress`, and `/api/summary` now carries `paths` and `suppressed`. Suppress only works on a server that has a token (403 otherwise); CSRF and token guards apply.
  2. Severity from the verdict. Red: `store/issue_verdict_test.go` (build: undefined IssueVerdict). Fix: `store/issues.go:110-166,294` (`RebuildIssuesWithVerdict`; `RebuildIssues` = no verdict) and `path_issues.go:298` (path severity = band). Rule applied: severity = max(min(risk band, scanner severity), best complete-path band). Exposures are not an input. Combinations keep their declared severity as a floor. DEVIATION from the literal "max(scanner, risk band, path band)": that formula can never lower a severity, so benign CRITICAL/HIGH could not drop. The risk band may only lower, and only a path raises. The existing `TestIssueCountsIncludePathIssues` expectation was updated (path BySeverity follows the band).
  3. Exposures. Red: `TestPathsPrintsExposures`, `TestAnalysisIncludesExposuresAndSuppressed` (suppressed null), and dashboard `TestRenderEmbedsExposures`. Fix: `paths.go:175` (block + `--exposures`); `server.go:751` (arrays, never null); `render.go:99,137` (top-level `exposures`/`suppressed`; Analysis is copied, not mutated).
  4. Gate. Red: `iac_unscanned_test.go`, the evade repro at `--fail-on critical` (exit 0) plus low/medium/info. Fix: `scan.go:764-789`. Gaps block every threshold through `output.GateBlocks`, and `--fail-on-unscanned=false` is the opt-out. `TestGateCountMatchesOutputGateCount` checks the gate equals `output.GateCount`.
  5. IAM switch-over. Red: 3 tests (the conditioned Deny * case, PassRole+RunInstances, and the new `policy_iam_switch_test.go`). Fix: `aws/policy.go:18,281`. `AdminLevel` calls `iam.IsAdmin`; each escalation action is judged on the collector's IAM ARN shapes (worst-of), so `arn:aws:iam::*:*` grants stay admin. The heuristic is kept only as a fallback when the parse fails. Expectations changed: conditioned Deny * → conditional; PassRole+RunInstances → full. No graph edge change; the node attrs `admin`/`admin_level` follow the evaluator.
  6. M365 token. Red: 307/308 sent the client secret to another origin, and https→http was followed. Fix: `saas/m365.go:307` `tokenRedirectSafe` refuses https downgrades (`core.ErrInsecureRedirect`) and any cross-origin hop (`ErrTokenRedirect`; the secret is in the body, so stripping headers is not enough). Same-origin redirects still work.
  7. Catalog. Red: `python/tests/test_catalog_sync.py` (alias drift). Fix: copied the Go catalog to `python/nimbus/frameworks/catalog.yaml`. pytest 235 passed.
  8. Scope. Red: `TestRebuildPathIssuesScoped` (build) and `TestPartialAccountScanKeepsOtherPathIssues`. The mutation re-run with a whole-estate scope gives `222222222222:resolved`. Fix: `store/path_issues.go:104,168,320` (`PathScope`, `RebuildPathIssuesScoped`). The CLI scopes every scan/ingest to the accounts of the nodes the scan itself produced (`scan.go:459`); prioritize scopes to the full stored graph.
  9–11 (V3-iac handoff). Red: `cli/fix_modules_test.go` (build: mergePatches arity). The mutation check with the old Address merge drops the second patch. Fix: `fix.go:436` `mergePatches` re-patches by `LocalAddress` and returns `dropped` (reported, never silent). `Callers` are printed ("callers: …"). `--all-callers` (`fix.go:79,245`) is wired; without it a shared source is refused, and the output names the callers and the flag. Unscanned contract: `fix.go:497` `unscannedFiles` refuses to patch a file in `UnscannedFiles` ("not scanned (reason)"), and `fix_verify.go:93` makes a re-scan where the patched file is unscanned come out unverified.
- Numbers (noise estate, `make groundtruth`): CRITICAL+HIGH open Issues 34 (6 TP-chain / 28 benign) → 30 (8 / 22). Path Issues 3 (4 paths). By severity: before C8 H26 M41 L24, after C8 H22 M35 L37. The drop is limited because the published risk score rates benign public website buckets / admin roles Critical (exposure ×1.4 wide-assumed, production ×1.3, identity ×1.4), so min(risk, scanner) rarely lowers them. Decision needed: cap single-Issue severity for resources the engine judged exposure-only / not on any path, or recalibrate the risk score. Main-lab rows P01–P12/F01/S01 are byte-identical to the baseline (precision 20/20).

### P2-REACH v1 result (Opus agent)

- `internal/reach` (new): a pure, deterministic evaluator, `Reachable(target, Internet(trusted...), port) Result{Reachable,
  Status, Width, Port, Sources, Path, Blockers, Unknown, Confidence}`. It models the address / RDS flag / LB scheme,
  the route table (longest-prefix match; IGW in, NAT/EIGW out only, TGW/PCX unknown), NACL in (ordered, first
  match), the SG, NACL out (ephemeral return ports), ALB/NLB listener → target group → target, Lambda URL,
  API GW, EKS CIDRs and service endpoints. It uses IPv4/IPv6 interval sets, and width is computed after every layer.
  Corpus: 70 cases in 7 `testdata/*.yaml` files, each with a docs source; F03 and F04 are included.
- Graph: `internal/graph/reachability.go`. `AnnotateReachability()` runs at the end of `AttachFindings` (and of the
  AWS analyzer). Keys on internet edges and exposed nodes: `reachability, reach_width, reach_cidrs, reach_port,
  reach_path, reach_blocker, reach_blocked_by, reach_unknown, reach_confidence` (+ `reach_via` on nodes). The account
  node carries `reach_coverage`. Helpers: `g.ReachabilityOf(id)` and `edge.Reachability()`. No new node types.
- AWS collector: route tables, NACLs, subnets, ENIs, IGW and NAT via EC2API paginators, plus a hand-rolled SigV4
  `ELBv2API` (`elbv2.go`). Each denied call is its own coverage row (`<region>:ec2:DescribeRouteTables`). New nodes:
  `aws_subnet` / `aws_network_acl` / `aws_lb`, LB → target `allows_traffic_to` edges with listener facts, SG
  `ingress_rules`, instance `public_ips`, RDS `subnet_ids`. `docs/iam/api-permissions.json` got 10 keys appended.
  `TestReadOnlyPolicies` was already current (pass 3 regenerated it).
- Lab, native ingest: F03 is blocked ("NACL acl-0a1b2c3d4e5f60001 rule 90 denies 22/tcp from 0.0.0.0/0", verified)
  and F04 is blocked ("route table rtb-0a1b2c3d4e5f60001 has no route to an internet gateway", verified). The ENGINE
  consumption is already live, so both are SUPPRESSED. I deleted their `lab/known_gaps.yaml` entries on the
  coordinator's instruction. `make groundtruth` passes in both normal and strict modes: precision 100% (28/28)
  labelled and real-world, recall 100% (20/20), recall@7 95%.
- Docs: `docs/reachability.md` (model, evidence format, limits, attackpath consumption).
- Deferred: TGW/peering/PrivateLink, prefix lists, DescribeRules (listener rules), ELB target group attributes,
  classic ELB, `ip` targets, API GW collection, Azure/GCP routes (v1 = NSG/firewall only → unknown).

Started. Bar: Microsoft Defender CSPM governance rules (owner, due by severity, grace, weekly owner email, on-time/overdue) + Jira/Datadog one ticket per Issue (assignee, due date, close follows the Issue). Edge: rules in the repo, CODEOWNERS fallback under the IaC root, `why` trail, MTTR from lifecycle history. Scope: internal/store, model/issue.go, cli, server, integrations (sinks + digest wiring), risk (SLA retirement only), dashboard {clients.js, render.go}, tools/ui-smoke check 25, docs. Not touched: collector/aws, reach, graph (REACH), governance package, git, `go mod tidy`.
Red first (all observed failing before the code): `internal/store/migrate7_test.go` (vet: `model.Issue.Owner` undefined), `internal/integrations/tickets_test.go` (vet: `IssueTicketer` undefined), `internal/cli/governance_test.go` (every Issue "not resolved": no owner_source/why/due), `internal/server/api_governance_test.go` (`overdue=1` ignored, returned every Issue), ui-smoke check 25 against a report from the pre-change binary ("payload has no governance metrics").
- Green. Store: migration 7 `issue_governance` (idempotent ALTERs: `owner, owner_source, owner_rule, due_at, grace_until, owner_why, sla_days, ticket_sink, ticket_key, ticket_url, ticket_state` + `idx_issues_owner(owner,state)`, `idx_issues_due(due_at,state)`); `store.SetIssueGovernance`, `SetIssueTicket`, `IssueFilter{Owner, OverdueOnly, Now}`; `model.Issue` governance + ticket fields, `Overdue` computed on read (open/reopened and past due; suppressed/resolved never), `IsOverdue`. Migration test on a v6 DB (md5 of v5 columns unchanged, re-apply no-op); migrate6 test now expects the latest version.
- CLI (`cli/governance.go`, `cli/digest.go`): `applyGovernance` after every Issue rebuild in scan/ingest (`finishScanWith`) and `prioritize`: resolver per CODEOWNERS root (config path or `.github/CODEOWNERS` under each IaC root, then cwd; file located under the IaC roots), account owner by name or identity (role_arn account id, subscription, project, tenant, org, domain, kube context), path Issues from the anchor node's name/owner tags, `CurrentEpisodeStart` per Issue; re-resolved every run (`TestGovernanceReResolvesOnEveryRun`). `issues --overdue/--owner`, DUE/OWNER/OVERDUE line per row, `issues metrics [--json]`, `issues show` owner (source, rule) / why / SLA / due / grace / OVERDUE / ticket. `prioritize` prints the governance due date ("no SLA" otherwise); `--owner` filters on the governance owner. `alert --digest [--since] [--dry-run] [--digest-sink slack|stdout]` (ledger `<db dir>/digest-ledger.json`, keys `<sink>/<owner>`; teams/email/webhook → "not implemented yet"). `alert` routes Jira/GitHub to `integrations.DispatchIssues`.
- Integrations (`tickets.go`): `IssueTicketer` (Jira, GitHubIssues), `DispatchIssues` — one ticket per Issue at/above the sink's min severity, idempotent via stored key (marker `cloud-posture-issue: <id>`, Jira label `cp-issue-<id>` as fallback), Jira assignee via `/rest/api/3/user/search` on an e-mail owner + `duedate`; close → transition to a `done` category, reopen → back + new duedate; GitHub `assignees` for `@user` owners, due date in body, close/reopen via PATCH; foreign-sink tickets skipped; suppressed left alone. `Result.IssueID`.
- Server: `GovernanceMetrics` / `BuildGovernanceMetrics` (MTTR/on-time from `CollectEvents`+`Metrics` with stored owner/SLA; open/overdue/past-grace from stored `due_at` so every surface agrees), `GovernRanked`, `AssignmentOf`; `/api/issues?overdue=1&owner=` (400 on a bad overdue value), `GET /api/governance/metrics`, `/api/risk/top` governed; Issue page `owner{owner,source,rule,why,sla_days,due_at,grace_until,overdue,past_grace}` + `ticket`.
- Risk: band SLA (7/30/90/180) removed from `internal/risk`; `Ranked.owner/sla_days/due_at` kept one release (deprecated, filled from governance); `go test ./internal/risk -run TestPythonGolden -update`; docs/scoring.md changelog + output-fields note. docs/governance.md "Using it" + what landed.
- Dashboard: `Data.Governance`; owners panel = overdue (drills to `q=overdue`, dead ends shown) · past grace · open, on-time rate, MTTR crit/high/med/low, open/overdue by owner (6 rows, `data-owner`); Issue rows DUE / OWNER columns with red `OVERDUE` badge; Issue pane owner (source, rule), why, grace, ticket state; issue filter matches owner and `overdue` (all words). ui-smoke check 25 (red on a pre-change report → green).
- Evidence: `go vet ./...` clean; `go test ./... -count=1` 39/39 ok; `pytest -q` 271 passed; `make groundtruth` PASS (lab precision 100% 28/28 both views, recall@7 95%; noise 4/4); `make ui-test` 26/26 + no page errors. Demo (scratchpad gov/): `issues metrics` open 51 · overdue 21 · on-time 100% (5/5), MTTR high 6.1d; screenshots `v4-owners-overview.png`, `v4-overdue-issues.png` (21 rows = panel's 21).

## Competitive delta review 2 (Opus reviewer, read-only on code)
Output: `docs/research/competitive-delta-2026-10-09-r2.md`. Evidence: scratch binary built at 23:09 (score 2.2.0; 220 MB, or 148 MB stripped / 36 MB gz). The lab was regenerated byte-identical and ingested into a temp DB (`scratchpad/delta2/`). Commands run: paths (+explain/exposures), plan, prioritize (explain/json), issues/show/metrics/--overdue, who-can, convert, scan --iac -o ocsf, mcp (initialize/tools/paths/issue/DELETE refused), pr-comment on bad/create/revert plans, alert --digest --dry-run, doctor, ingest --diff, first run in an empty HOME. Harnesses: make ui-test (25 + no errors), groundtruth PASS (lab 28/28 both views, recall@7 95%; noise 4/4), dashboard screenshots, Python copilot offline (5 questions), make readme-check (**FAIL**: 3 bad anchors `docs/benchmark.md#cloud-lab-unverified`).
Round-1 top 15: 7 CLOSED (saturation 40 → 4 at 10.0, Issue page, who-can/mcp/convert wired, escalation gate exit 3, remediation phases, KEV/EPSS gate, identity chains at 7.0), 5 IMPROVED (one band per path, but single Issues use scanner severity; F03/F04 blocked in paths; Trivy 0 → 22/44 mapped; CLI paths grouped; governance), 3 OPEN (release, verify, rule authoring). Table of 91 rows: BEATS 3 → 4 (AP10 added), MATCHES 16 → 28, IN-FLIGHT 8 → 0; 18 rows up, 1 down (AI1 copilot re-scored).
New top shortfalls: (1) F04 ledger still `prioritize` #7 at 9.8 Critical with "internet exposed (wide)", the Issue CRITICAL and the copilot calling it exposed: REACH stops at `paths`; (2) 7 rule-chain Issues duplicate traversal Issues at different scores; (3) no release; (4) no live run; (5) `who-can` omits anonymous access on the public PII bucket; (6) no verify, and `--diff` hides path score drops; (7) dashboard paths view shows 28 raw variants and the fan graph; (8) copilot contradicts the engine; (9) 70/89 Issues unassigned by default; (10) stale README/help/doctor and readme-check red; (11) `scan` with no targets prints "0 open findings" and exits 0; (12) P13 has no fix template; (13) rule authoring; (14) PR comment gives no de-escalation credit and no suggestion blocks; (15) about half of Trivy/Checkov findings unmapped.
Verdict: the engine is ahead of every named competitor in substance on the wedge capabilities, but not best-in-class to a Wiz-trained CISO yet. The single change that would flip it: a published live-account run, after #1 (S) lands. The doc includes a v0.2.0 release-readiness checklist (16 items; 3 met).

### Security (sub-agent, tree-sec)
| item | verdict | excerpt |
|---|---|---|
| MCP protocol / SQL / scope / audit mode | VERIFIED | smoke exit 0. ATTACH, PRAGMA, `;DELETE`, CTE-DELETE, VACUUM INTO, load_extension, readfile and writefile are all rejected. Recursive CTE hits the 10 s timeout. A 50 MB line gives "exceeds the 4 MiB limit". A batch gives -32600. A call before init gives -32002. A hidden tool gives "outside this session's scope". The audit file is 0600, and newlines are JSON-escaped. draft_fix `../../../etc/passwd` gives "unknown format" |
| MCP redaction | **FAILED** | `draft_fix {"finding_id":"ACCT_…"}` → `no finding with id "111111111111"` (confirmed in logs/mcp_draft.log). `SELECT * FROM "ACCT_…"` → `no such table: 111111111111`. `hex()`, `substr`, `CAST(account AS INTEGER)`, `account\|\|'x'`, `group_concat`, `tags AS t` and LIKE oracles all leak |
| MCP audit symlink | PARTIAL | appends to the symlink target and chmods it |
| plan diff hostile plans | PARTIAL | The limits hold. The plan is never read as file paths. `<img onerror>`, `](javascript:)`, backticks, the spoofed marker, `@org/team` and `#123` are all entity-escaped. Redact defaults to on. Cross-host `Link: next` is not followed. **Breaks:** with an empty `--bot-login` (the default), a human comment that starts with the marker gets PATCHed. The action.yml guard is a denylist |
| governance sinks | **FAILED** | No `CheckRedirect` in internal/integrations (confirmed). A cross-host 307 forwards `DD-API-KEY` and custom headers. Jira Basic and GitHub Bearer go out over an https→http downgrade. There is no URL validation, so a request is sent to 169.254.169.254 |
| convert hostile → html/csv | VERIFIED | JSON island with `<`/` ` escaped, no innerHTML/href sinks. CSV formula prefixing works. A depth bomb is refused. Low: no file size cap (a 200 MB file → 1.39 GB RSS) |
| Python copilot | PARTIAL | 27 AI tests pass. Five off-list tool names are refused, and the wrapper is not escaped. **Leak:** aliasing (`hex(account) AS title`, `CAST(account AS INTEGER)`, `tags AS title`) sends raw account ids and tags to the provider |
| ELBv2 / Organizations signed clients | PARTIAL | No InsecureSkipVerify. 16/4 MiB response caps. 30 s timeout. `Authorization` is stripped on redirect, but `X-Amz-Security-Token` is forwarded. A repeated NextToken loops (~27k req in 2 s) until the context deadline |
| issues suppress | PARTIAL | Correct behaviour: 401/403/421/400/404, Origin and Sec-Fetch checks, ConstantTimeCompare. Accepted: expiry `9999-12-31`, a 900 KB reason, a client-asserted `approved_by` |
| read-only policy `-update` diff | VERIFIED | empty diff on both files. 49 read-only actions, including all 7 hand-rolled ELBv2/Organizations actions. No write actions |

### New defects (severity, location, repro)
1. HIGH — MCP placeholder round trip leaks real ids through error text. `mcp/server.go:336-341` restores the placeholders, then `:356` returns the raw error. Repro: `SELECT account …` → `ACCT_x`; `draft_fix {"finding_id":"ACCT_x"}`.
2. HIGH — MCP redaction is defeated by computed SQL values (numbers, hex, substr, concat, aliasing, LIKE oracle). `mcp/redact.go:85-119`. At minimum, redact numeric values and error strings, and document that redaction does not hold against a hostile client.
3. MEDIUM — Python copilot outbound redaction is bypassed by column aliasing and integers. `python/nimbus/ai/redact.py:74,109`. Repro: `tree-sec/python/tests/test_zz_verify4_injection.py`.
4. MEDIUM — Integration sinks leak credentials on redirect (DD-API-KEY, custom headers cross-host; Jira/GitHub auth over a downgrade), and there is no SSRF guard. `integrations/integrations.go:34`, `datadog.go:69`, `tickets.go:257`. Repro: `tree-sec/internal/integrations/zz_verify4_redirect_test.go`.
5. MEDIUM — Sticky comment hijack: `BotLogin` is empty by default (`gitops/gitops.go:313`, `cli/prcomment.go:68`), so a human comment with the marker gets PATCHed. Repro: `TestZZV4StickyHumanMarker`.
6. MEDIUM — action.yml:84 uses a denylist (only `pull_request_target`). `workflow_run` and `issue_comment` pass. It should allowlist events instead.
7. MEDIUM — An IAM SCP Deny applies to principals the evaluator itself treats as SCP-exempt, which under-reports. `iam/evaluate.go:284` vs :292/:304.
8. MEDIUM — Reach: an SG prefix-list source becomes blocked/verified, dropping live entries (`graph/reachability.go:658-700`). An IPv6-only instance is blocked instead of unknown (`reach/eval.go:631`). Spurious blocker evidence (`eval.go:573`).
9. MEDIUM — D3: the governance clock is not reset when a suppression expires on single/combination Issues. `store/issues.go:309` vs `path_issues.go:414`.
10. MEDIUM-HIGH — D4: path Issue provider/account flip with ingest order. `graph/graph.go:96-157`, `attackpath/traversal.go:1104-1110`, `store/path_issues.go:309`. Affects `--provider/--account` filters and owner-by-account.
11. MEDIUM — D1/D2: trust conditions are never read (the `external_id_required` attribute has no producer), so same-org and ExternalId trusts get floored to 7.00. `attackpath/traversal.go:590-625`, `identity.go:63-80`.
12. MEDIUM — D8: `0.0.0.0/1 + 128.0.0.0/1` counts as narrow (`collector/core/exposure.go:98-104`, confirmed), so the path is 1.90 Low.
13. MEDIUM — D5: plan-diff misses a new exposed workload on an anchor that is already reachable (`model/issue.go:120-127`).
14. LOW — R14 prefix-list route blocked (should be unknown); R28 tcp 0–0 = all ports; SLR session not recognised; D6 rename trips `--opened-only`; D7 dedup boundary; `clip` byte cut leaves U+FFFD (`digest.go:287`); suppression with no max expiry / no reason cap / spoofable `approved_by` (`path_issues.go:383`, `server.go:672`); audit follows symlinks (`mcp/audit.go:59,63`); signed clients forward `X-Amz-Security-Token` cross-host and loop on a repeated NextToken (`elbv2.go:102,177,218`, `scp.go:168,212,234`); `convert` has no file size cap; IaC depth guard ~1.9 GB RSS on a 2 MiB file; `mcp` writes views into the DB at startup.
15. LOW (gate/doc) — `make readme-check` red (stale anchor); README.md:67 stale; docs/benchmark.md does not disclose that the noise estate was used for tuning; docs/scoring.md:347 says "5/62 at 10.0, Critical 22" but the test now logs 4 and 21; docs/plan-diff.md:98 / action.yml do not mention escalations tripping the gate or `opened-only`; 3 new gofmt files.

- "Lab prioritize: 10.0 ties 41 → 5 /62, Critical 46 → 22" (docs/scoring.md:347) now measures 4 / 21.
- docs/benchmark.md calls the noise estate "the stronger evidence for precision". After the noise-round tuning it is in-sample, not held-out evidence.
- Every other numeric claim checked was reproduced: groundtruth numbers, golden coverage gate, ui-smoke 25, pytest 271, read-only policy current, P12/P13/P15/P18/P20 at 7.00, F02/F03/F04/F09/S01 suppressed.

## Verification 4 → wave 4 (main session)
V4 found 2 HIGH (MCP redaction leaks via error text and via SQL functions), mediums (nondeterministic provider/account on path Issues, Python copilot outbound redaction, sink HTTP client redirects/SSRF, sticky-comment hijack, action.yml event allowlist, SCP exemption order, reach blocked-vs-unknown cases, trust conditions ignored, /1 CIDR union narrow, suppression SLA clock), lows; functional claims held (IAM 63/65 adversarial, reach 27/31, floors 8/8, scoring explain 165/165). Benchmark caveat: noise precision is in-sample after tuning — must be stated.
Wave 4 launched (disjoint): V4-SEC (all security defects + lows), V4-ENGINE (verdict into risk/Issue/copilot helper, rule-chain dedup, determinism, SCP order, reach fixes, trust conditions, CIDR union, anonymous principal in who-can, PassRole fix template, benchmark in-sample caveat), V4-PRODUCT (`verify` command with targeted re-collect + backoff, dashboard grouped paths, copilot parity, onboarding ownership rules, empty-scan exit 2, PR de-escalation credit + suggestions, alias batch ≥70%, docs truth + `make readme-bench`). Next: gate → Verification 5 → delta review 3; release v0.2.0 when O3/O4 land.

- Gate on the live tree (`scratchpad/v4sec/gate.log`): `go vet ./...` exit 0. `go test ./internal/mcp/ ./internal/integrations/ ./internal/gitops/ ./internal/collector/... ./internal/output/ ./internal/store/ ./internal/iac/ ./internal/cli/ -count=1` gives 14 ok, exit 0. Also green: `./internal/server`, `./internal/governance`. `pytest -q`: 301 passed, 1 failed. The failure is `test_copilot_parity.py::test_copilot_agrees_with_paths_on_the_lab` (`KeyError 'status_agreeing'`): V4-PRODUCT's in-flight copilot/parity edit (both files modified 23:53–23:54), not V4-SEC. Earlier during this track, `internal/mcp/verdict_test.go` (another agent) was red until that agent's `tools.go` change landed. My `tools.go` edits survived the merge.
- MCP end to end on a copy of the verify-4 lab DB (`mcp_e2e.log`): all 37 bypass and oracle queries return 0 leaks. `draft_fix(ACCT_x)` gives `no finding with id "ACCT_x"`. `FROM "ACCT_x"` gives `no such table: ACCT_x`.
- Residual risks (honest):
  - MCP: an identifier in a shape the redactor does not recognise (account id with dashes, hostname, e-mail, a tag value copied into free text such as a lab `description`) is visible and computable. Placeholder equality is an intended input. Redaction minimises what an assistant sees; it is not an access-control boundary. The CTE prefix counts against the 64 KiB SQL limit; a query that names many large views can hit it.
  - Python: the function denylist covers model-chosen SQL in the agent path. The `ask()` path relies on value-level checks only, and its SQL comes from the user's own question. A yes/no oracle through equality on non-account columns is not blocked (bounded by the turn budget).
  - Sinks: the sink client no longer uses `HTTPS_PROXY`, because a proxy would defeat the connect-time IP check. `--allow-private-sinks` is not yet wired as a CLI flag (`cli/root.go`, V4-PRODUCT); env `NIMBUS_ALLOW_PRIVATE_SINKS=1` works.
  - IaC guard: a window that lies entirely inside one quoted/heredoc template doubles until it does not. A crafted file whose whole 2 MiB is one dense template can still cost a full lex (no crash; the size cap holds).
  - Sticky comment: outside Actions, a GitHub App installation token needs `--bot-login` (clear error, no edit).

## V4-SEC done (main session note)
MCP: view-level redaction (same-named placeholder views; raw ids never reach SQLite functions; LIKE oracle blind; errors redacted); Python copilot redaction + guardrail denylist; sink URL policy (https only, no metadata/private ranges, connect-time IP check, redirect credential stripping); sticky comment edits only the bot's own comments; action.yml event allowlist; suppression bounds; audit O_NOFOLLOW; AWS signed clients no cross-host token + page-loop stop; convert 64 MiB cap; IaC guard windowed lexing (1,144 MB → ~14 MB). Residual risks documented. Handoffs (approved_by from principal, --allow-private-sinks, convert cap wiring, governance CollectEvents) sent to V4-PRODUCT.
